**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/image-analyst/computechange.html

**text**　本示例用于計算 2000 年和 2020 年特定分類土地覆被類的差異。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/image-analyst/computechange.html#code-sample

## ComputeChange · 代碼示例

**id**　c4ea49d287faa0fc3a-15

**english**　ComputeChange

**chapter**　
- Python
- 影像分析模塊
- 函數
- 分析

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/image-analyst/computechange.html#code-sample

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/image-analyst/computechange.html

**text**　import arcpy
from arcpy.ia import *

raster1 = arcpy.Raster('Landcover_2000.tif')
raster2 = arcpy.Raster('Landcover_2020.tif')

# Compute only the change that occured in land cover between 2000 and 2020
changed_landcover = arcpy.ia.ComputeChange(raster1, raster2,
    "CATEGORICAL_DIFFERENCE",[41,42,43],[21,22,23],"CHANGED_PIXELS_ONLY",
    "AVERAGE","IntersectionOf","MaxOf")

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/image-analyst/computechange.html#code-sample

## GetImageEXIFProperties · 返回值

**id**　c50a722f9d1c5b34db-5

**english**　GetImageEXIFProperties

**chapter**　
- Python
- ArcPy 函數
- 網格

**kind**　表格

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/functions/getimageexifproperties.html#return-value

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/functions/getimageexifproperties.html

**parameter**　

**interface**　

**headers**　
- 資料類型
- 說明

**values**　
- List
- 輸出元資料。 經度和緯度將以十進制度為單位，高度將以圖像 EXIF 中定義的單位表示。 經度、緯度和高度將在列表的前三個索引位置返回。使用以下 EXIF 標記作為鍵，EXIF 屬性將在下一位置作為字典返回。 EXIF_DateTimeOriginal EXIF_FocalLength EXIF_FocalLengthIn35mmFilm EXIF_PixelXDimension EXIF_PixelYDimension EXIF_FocalPlaneResolutionUnit EXIF_FocalPlaneXResolution EXIF_FocalPlaneYResolution EXIF_Model EXIF_Make EXIF_BlackLevelRepeatDim EXIF_BlackLevel EXIF_GPSImgDirection EXIF_GPSImgDirectionRef EXIF_Orientation EXIF 屬性將以 XMP 格式返回。 如果找不到有效的 EXIF 屬性信息，則該函數將返回 None 。 GetImageEXIFProperties 示例輸出 返回 JPEG 圖像的 EXIF 屬性。 [7.172077777777778, 50.68396666666666, 60.0, {'width': 2592, 'height': 1944, 'planes': 3, 'EXIF_KIND': 'IMAGE', 'EXIF_BAND_COUNT': 3, 'EXIF_HAS_TABLE': False, 'EXIF_HAS_XFORM': False, 'EXIF_DateTimeOriginal': '2009:01:13 11:38:12', 'EXIF_FocalLength': 11.3, 'EXIF_PixelXDimension': 2592, 'EXIF_PixelYDimension': 1944, 'EXIF_Model': 'Caplio 500SE ', 'EXIF_Make': 'RICOH ', 'EXIF_GPSImgDirection': 27.7, 'EXIF_GPSImgDirectionRef': 'M', 'EXIF_Orientation': '1'}]

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/functions/getimageexifproperties.html#return-value

## FromCoordString · 語法

**id**　c52c52e3682b8ffb06-5

**english**　FromCoordString

**chapter**　
- Python
- ArcPy 函數
- 幾何

**kind**　表格

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/functions/fromcoordstring.html#syntax

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/functions/fromcoordstring.html

**parameter**　

**interface**　

**headers**　
- 參數
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- notation
- Specifies the coordinate system notation of the input string. DD— Decimal degrees is used, for example, 34.05719570N 117.19647020W. DDM— Degrees decimal minutes is used, for example, 34 03.43174200N 117 11.78821200W. DMS— Degree Minute Seconds, is used, for example, 34 03 25.90452000N 117 11 47.29272000W. GARS— Global Area Reference System is used, for example, 126LJ47. It is based on latitude and longitude, and it divides and subdivides the world into cells. GEOREF— World Geographic Reference System is used, for example, EJCE4821203432. MGRS— Military Grid Reference System is used, for example, 11SMT8186968515. USNG— United States National Grid is used, for example, 11S MT 81869 68515. UTM— Universal Transverse Mercator is used, for example, 11S 481868 3768515. It is based on zone number, MGRS latitude band, and the easting and northing planar coordinate pair in that zone. UTMNS— Universal Transverse Mercator (no spaces) is used, for example, 11N4818683768515. It is based on zone number, hemisphere designator, and the easting and northing planar coordinate pair in that zone.
- String

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/functions/fromcoordstring.html#syntax

**interface**

- 參數
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- extent_type
- Specifies the processing extent for the output raster. FirstOf—Use the extent of the first input raster to determine the processing extent. IntersectionOf—Use the extent of the overlapping pixels to determine the processing extent. This is the default. UnionOf—Use the extent of all the rasters to determine the processing extent. LastOf—Use the extent of the last input raster to determine the processing extent. (默認值為 IntersectionOf)
- String

- 資料類型

**values**　
- cellsize_type
- Specifies the cell size to use in the output raster. FirstOf—Use the first cell size of the input rasters. This is the default. MinOf—Use the smallest cell size of all the input rasters. MaxOf—Use the largest cell size of all the input rasters. This is the default. MeanOf—Use the mean cell size of all the input rasters. LastOf—Use the last cell size of the input rasters. (默認值為 MaxOf)
- String

**content_version**　3.7

## GeometricMedian · 代碼示例

**id**　c53c1c3e743b5d7978-11

**english**　GeometricMedian

**chapter**　
- Python
- 影像分析模塊
- 函數
- 資料管理

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/image-analyst/geometricmedian.html#code-sample

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/image-analyst/geometricmedian.html

**text**　# Import system modules
import arcpy
from arcpy.ia import *

# Construct a collection from a mosaic dataset
rc = RasterCollection(r'C:\data.gdb\time_series_landsat_images')

# Run
geomedian = arcpy.ia.GeometricMedian(rc, {"epsilon": 0.001, "max_iteration": 5, "extent_type":"UnionOf", "CellsizeType": "FirstOf"})

# Save the output
geomedian.save("C:/arcpyExamples/outputs/geomedian_raster.crf")

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/image-analyst/geometricmedian.html#code-sample

## Mask · 語法

**id**　c5714032d673a0e0b8-7

**english**　Mask

**chapter**　
- Python
- 影像分析模塊
- 函數
- 資料管理

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/image-analyst/mask.html#syntax

**parameter**　

**interface**　

**headers**　
- 參數
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- no_data_interpretation
- 指定 NoData 值在輸出網格中的顯示方式。 0—所有 - 為各波段指定的 NoData 值必須出現在輸出影像的同一像元中，以包含 NoData 像元。 1—任意 - 如果指定的 NoData 值出現在指定波段的像元中，則輸出影像中的該像元將是 NoData。 (默認值為 None)
- Integer

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/image-analyst/mask.html#syntax

## SubnetLine 屬性的聚合線 · 代碼示例

**id**　c5d8b0a32074f89ef8-5

**english**　Aggregated Lines for SubnetLine properties

**chapter**　
- Python
- ArcPy 函數
- 描述資料
- Describe 屬性
- 公共設施網絡屬性
- 域網絡屬性
- 層

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/functions/aggregated-lines-for-subnetline.html#code-sample

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/functions/aggregated-lines-for-subnetline.html

**text**　# Import required modules
import arcpy

# Describe functions on a Utility Network
UN = "C:\\Projects\\MyProject\\unowner.sde\\Naperville.UNOWNER.Naperville\\Naperville.UNOWNER.Naperville"
d = arcpy.Describe(UN)

# Domain Network properties
domnets = d.domainNetworks

# For each domain network in the utility network
for dom in domnets:
    print(f"Domain Network Name: {dom.domainNetworkName}")

    # For each tier in the domain network
    for tier in dom.tiers:
        print(f"Tier Name: {tier.name}")

        # Aggregated Lines for SubnetLine Properties
        for al in tier.aggregatedLinesForSubnetLine:
            print(" -- Aggregated Lines for SubnetLine Properties -- ")
            print(f"Asset Group Code: {al.assetGroupCode} \n")
            print(" - Asset Type Properties - ")
            for at in al.assetTypes:
                print(f"Asset Type Code: {at.assetTypeCode} \n")

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/functions/aggregated-lines-for-subnetline.html#code-sample

## SearchCursor · 討論

**id**　c64e5bf837e4523be0-2

**english**　SearchCursor

**chapter**　
- Python
- 資料訪問模塊
- 類

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/data-access/searchcursor-class.html#discussion

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/data-access/searchcursor-class.html

**text**　Geometry 對象屬性可通過在欄位列表中指定令牌 SHAPE@ 進行訪問。
使用 for 循環可迭代搜索游標。 搜索游標也支持 with 語句以重置迭代并幫助移除鎖。 但是，為了防止鎖定所有內容，應考慮使用 del 語句來刪除對象或將游標包含在函數中以使游標對象位于作用范圍之外。
利用屬性條件或空間條件，可以限制 SearchCursor 返回的記錄。
使用 SHAPE@ 訪問整個幾何是一種代價較高的操作。 如果只需幾何信息（如點的 x,y 座標），請使用 SHAPE@XY 、 SHAPE@Z 和 SHAPE@M 等令牌進行更快速、更高效的訪問。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/data-access/searchcursor-class.html#discussion

## SearchCursor · 語法

**id**　c64e5bf837e4523be0-5

**english**　SearchCursor

**chapter**　
- Python
- 資料訪問模塊
- 類

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/data-access/searchcursor-class.html#syntax

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/data-access/searchcursor-class.html

**parameter**　field_names

**interface**　

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- field_names
- 欄位名稱列表（或組）。 對于單個欄位，可以使用一個字符串，而不使用字符串列表。 要訪問輸入表中的所有欄位（BLOB 欄位除外），可以使用星號 (*) 代替欄位列表。 但是，為了獲得較快的性能和可靠的欄位順序，建議您將欄位列表限制在實際需要的欄位。 以令牌（如 OID@ ）取代欄位名稱可訪問更多的信息： ANNO@—圖徵的 Annotation 對象。 此選項僅對 ArcGIS Pro 注記圖徵類 有效。 SHAPE@XY—一組圖徵的質心 x,y 座標。 SHAPE@XYZ—一組圖徵的質心 x,y,z 座標。 SHAPE@TRUECENTROID—一組圖徵的質心 x,y 座標。 這會返回與 SHAPE@XY 相同的值。 SHAPE@X—圖徵的雙精度 x 座標。 SHAPE@Y—圖徵的雙精度 y 座標。 SHAPE@Z—圖徵的雙精度 z 座標。 SHAPE@M—圖徵的雙精度 m 值。 SHAPE@JSON—表示幾何的 Esri JSON 字符串。 SHAPE@WKB—OGC 幾何的熟知二進制 (WKB) 表示。 用于以可移植的方式將幾何值表示為連續的字節流。值返回為 bytearray 對象。 SHAPE@WKT—OGC 幾何的熟知文本 (WKT) 表示。 用于以可移植的方式將幾何值表示為文本字符串。 SHAPE@—圖徵的 幾何 對象。 SHAPE@AREA—圖徵的雙精度面積。 SHAPE@LENGTH—圖徵的雙精度長度。 CREATED@—圖徵創建時間的日期時間對象。 此欄位為只讀欄位。 CREATOR@—創建圖徵的用戶名字符串。 此欄位為只讀欄位。 EDITED@—圖徵上次編輯時間的日期時間對象。 此欄位為只讀欄位。 EDITOR@—上次編輯圖徵的用戶名字符串。 此欄位為只讀欄位。 GLOBALID@—圖徵的通用唯一標識符字符串。 此欄位為只讀欄位。 OID@—“對象 ID”欄位的值。 SUBTYPE@—整型子類型編碼。
- String

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/data-access/searchcursor-class.html#syntax

- 用于限制所返回的記錄的可選表達式。 有關 WHERE 子句和 SQL 語句的詳細信息，請參閱 在 ArcGIS 中使用的查詢表達式的 SQL 參考 。 (默認值為 None)
- String

## SearchCursor · 語法

**id**　c64e5bf837e4523be0-7

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/data-access/searchcursor-class.html#syntax

**parameter**　spatial_reference

**interface**　

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- spatial_reference （可選）
- 圖徵類的空間參考。 指定此參數后，將根據輸入的空間參考對圖徵進行投影（或變換）。 如果未指定，則將使用輸入圖徵類的空間參考。 此參數的有效值為 SpatialReference 對象或等效字符串。 If a spatial reference is specified, but the input feature class has an unknown spatial reference, neither a projection nor transformation can be completed. The geometry returned by the cursor will have coordinates matching the input, with a spatial reference updated to the one specified. (默認值為 None)
- SpatialReference

## SearchCursor · 語法

**id**　c64e5bf837e4523be0-9

**english**　SearchCursor

**chapter**　
- Python
- 資料訪問模塊
- 類

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/data-access/searchcursor-class.html#syntax

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/data-access/searchcursor-class.html

**parameter**　sql_clause

**interface**　

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- sql_clause （可選）
- 以列表或組的形式列出的 SQL 前綴和后綴子句對。 SQL 前綴子句支持 None 、 DISTINCT 和 TOP 。 SQL 后綴子句支持 None 、 ORDER BY 和 GROUP BY 。 Use DISTINCT in a prefix clause. with arcpy.da.SearchCursor( in_features, ["OID@", "STREET_NAME"], sql_clause=("DISTINCT STREET_NAME", None) ) as cur: print('Additional code') Use TOP in a prefix clause, and ORDER BY in a postfix clause. with arcpy.da.SearchCursor( in_features, ['OID@', "ELEVATION"], sql_clause=("TOP 5", "ORDER BY ELEVATION DESC") ) as cur: print('Additional code') Use GROUP BY in a postfix clause. with arcpy.da.SearchCursor( in_features, ['STREET_NAME'], sql_clause=(None, "GROUP BY STREET_NAME") ) as cur: print('Additional code') SQL 前綴子句位于第一個位置，并將被插入到 SELECT 關鍵字與 SELECT COLUMN LIST 之間。 SQL 前綴子句最常用于 DISTINCT 或 ALL 等子句。 SQL 后綴子句位于第二個位置，并將被追加到 where 子句之后的 SELECT 語句中。 SQL 后綴子句最常用于 ORDER BY 等子句。 注: 僅在使用資料庫時支持 DISTINCT 、 ORDER BY 和 ALL 。 它們不適用于其他資料源（如 dBASE 或 INFO 表）。 僅 SQL Server 資料庫支持 TOP 。 (默認值為 (None, None))
- tuple

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/data-access/searchcursor-class.html#syntax

- 用于對圖徵進行空間過濾的幾何對象。 如果指定此參數，則游標將基于指定的幾何和 spatial_relationship 值來限制返回的圖徵。 (默認值為 None)

## SearchCursor · 語法

**id**　c64e5bf837e4523be0-12

**english**　SearchCursor

**chapter**　
- Python
- 資料訪問模塊
- 類

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/data-access/searchcursor-class.html#syntax

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/data-access/searchcursor-class.html

**parameter**　spatial_relationship

**interface**　

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- spatial_relationship （可選）
- spatial_filter 參數中的輸入和查詢幾何之間的空間關系。 僅當指定 spatial_filter 參數時，此參數才適用。 INTERSECTS— Rows are only returned when the spatial_filter geometry intersects the input row's geometry. ENVELOPE_INTERSECTS— Rows are only returned when the spatial_filter geometry's envelope intersects the input row's geometry. INDEX_INTERSECTS— Rows are only returned when the spatial_filter geometry's envelope intersects the index entry for the input row's geometry. Because it uses the underlying index grid, rather than the envelope of the feature, it is faster and is commonly used for return features for display purposes. TOUCHES— Rows are only returned when the spatial_filter geometry touches the input row's geometry. OVERLAPS— Rows are only returned when the spatial_filter geometry overlaps the input row's geometry. CROSSES— Rows are only returned when the spatial_filter geometry crosses the input row's geometry. WITHIN— Rows are only returned when the spatial_filter geometry is within the input row's geometry. CONTAINS— Rows are only returned when the spatial_filter geometry contains the input row's geometry. (默認值為 INTERSECTS)
- String

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/data-access/searchcursor-class.html#syntax

**id**　c64e5bf837e4523be0-17

**text**　import arcpy

fc = 'c:/data/base.gdb/well'
fields = ['WELL_ID', 'WELL_TYPE', 'SHAPE@XY']

# For each row, print the WELL_ID and WELL_TYPE fields, and
# the feature's x,y coordinates
with arcpy.da.SearchCursor(fc, fields) as cursor:
    for row in cursor:
        print(f'{row[0]}, {row[1]}, {row[2]}')

## SearchCursor · 代碼示例

**id**　c64e5bf837e4523be0-23

**english**　SearchCursor

**chapter**　
- Python
- 資料訪問模塊
- 類

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/data-access/searchcursor-class.html#method-reset

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/data-access/searchcursor-class.html

**text**　import arcpy

fc = 'c:/base/data.gdb/roads'
class_field = 'Road Class'
name_field = 'Name'

# Create an expression with proper delimiters
delimited_field = arcpy.AddFieldDelimiters(fc, name_field)
expression = f'{delimited_field} = 2'

# Create a search cursor using an SQL expression
with arcpy.da.SearchCursor(
    fc, [class_field, name_field], where_clause=expression
) as cursor:
    for row in cursor:
        # Print the name of the residential road
        print(row[1])

**text**　import arcpy

fc = 'c:/data/base.gdb/well'
fields = ['WELL_ID', 'WELL_TYPE']

# Use ORDER BY sql clause to sort field values
with arcpy.da.SearchCursor(fc, fields, sql_clause=(None, "ORDER BY WELL_ID, WELL_TYPE")) as cursor:
    for row in cursor:
        print(f'{row[0]}, {row[1]}')

**text**　import arcpy

arr = arcpy.Array(
    [arcpy.Point(342917.4, 553980.8), arcpy.Point(366915.9, 594749.1)]
)
new_road = arcpy.Polyline(arr, spatial_reference=arcpy.SpatialReference(26971))

fc = r"C:\data\chicago.gdb\houses"
fields = ["ADDRESS", "OCCUPIED"]

with arcpy.da.SearchCursor(
    fc, fields, where_clause="OCCUPIED != 'Vacant'", spatial_filter=new_road
) as cursor:
    for row in cursor:
        print(f'{row[0]}: {row[1]}')

## Speckle · 語法

**id**　c6750913562f753444-5

**english**　Speckle

**chapter**　
- Python
- Spatial Analyst 模塊
- 函數
- 校正

**kind**　表格

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/spatial-analyst/speckle.html#syntax

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/spatial-analyst/speckle.html

**parameter**　

**interface**　

**headers**　
- 參數
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- filter_type
- Specifies the filter type that will be used in the smoothing algorithm to remove speckle noise. Lee—The Lee filter will be used, which reduces the speckle noise by applying a spatial filter to each pixel in an image and filters the data based on local statistics calculated within a square window. EnhancedLee—The EnhancedLee filter will be used, which is a refined version of the Lee filter that reduces the speckle noise effectively by preserving image sharpness and detail. This filter requires the damp_factor and nlooks arguments to be set. Frost—The Frost filter will be used, which reduces speckle noise and preserves important image features at the edges with an exponentially damped circularly symmetric filter that uses local statistics within individual filter windows. This filter requires the damp_factor argument to be set. Kuan—The Kuan filter will be used, which follows a similar filtering process to the Lee filter in reducing speckle noise. This filter also applies a spatial filter to each pixel in an image, filtering the data based on local statistics of the centered pixel value that is calculated using the neighboring pixels. The Kuan filter requires the nlooks argument to be set, which controls image smoothing and estimates noise variance. Gamma MAP—A Bayesian analysis and gamma distribution filter will be used to reduce the speckle noise. This filter is useful for reducing speckle while preserving edges. Refined Lee—A spatial filter will be applied to selected pixels, based on local statistics, to reduce the speckle noise. This filter uses a nonsquare filter window to match the direction of edges. It is useful for reducing speckle while preserving edges. (默認值為 Lee)
- String

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/spatial-analyst/speckle.html#syntax

## Speckle · 語法

**id**　c6750913562f753444-6

**english**　Speckle

**chapter**　
- Python
- Spatial Analyst 模塊
- 函數
- 校正

**kind**　表格

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/spatial-analyst/speckle.html#syntax

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/spatial-analyst/speckle.html

**parameter**　

**interface**　

**headers**　
- 參數
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- filter_size
- Specifies the size of the filter window in pixels. 3x3—The pixel size of the filter window is 3 by 3. 5x5—The pixel size of the filter window is 5 by 5. 7x7—The pixel size of the filter window is 7 by 7. 9x9—The pixel size of the filter window is 9 by 9. 11x11—The pixel size of the filter window is 11 by 11. (默認值為 3x3)
- String

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/spatial-analyst/speckle.html#syntax

## Speckle · 語法

**id**　c6750913562f753444-7

**english**　Speckle

**chapter**　
- Python
- Spatial Analyst 模塊
- 函數
- 校正

**kind**　表格

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/spatial-analyst/speckle.html#syntax

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/spatial-analyst/speckle.html

**parameter**　

**interface**　

**headers**　
- 參數
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- noise_model
- Specifies the type of noise that is reducing the quality of the radar image. This argument is only valid when the filter_type argument is set to Lee. Multiplicative—Random signal noise that is multiplied into the relevant signal during capture or transmission is reducing the quality of the image. Additive—Random signal noise that is added into the relevant signal during capture or transmission is reducing the quality of the image. AdditiveAndMultiplicative—A combination of both noise models is reducing the quality of the image. (默認值為 Multiplicative)
- String

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/spatial-analyst/speckle.html#syntax

## Speckle · 語法

**id**　c6750913562f753444-8

**english**　Speckle

**chapter**　
- Python
- Spatial Analyst 模塊
- 函數
- 校正

**kind**　表格

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/spatial-analyst/speckle.html#syntax

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/spatial-analyst/speckle.html

**parameter**　

**interface**　

**headers**　
- 參數
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- noise_var
- The noise variance of the radar image. This argument is only valid when the filter_type argument is set to Lee and the noise_model argument is set to Additive or AdditiveAndMultiplicative. (默認值為 None)
- Double

**source_language**　zh-cn

**id**　c6750913562f753444-11

**english**　Speckle

**chapter**

- Spatial Analyst 模塊

**interface**　

**headers**　
- 參數
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- nlooks
- The number of looks of the image, which controls image smoothing and estimates noise variance. A smaller value results in more smoothing, while a larger value retains more image features. This argument is only valid when the filter_type argument is set to Lee and the noise_model argument is set to Multiplicative, or when the filter_type argument is set to Kuan. (默認值為 1)
- Integer

**interface**

- 參數
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- damp_factor
- The extent of exponential damping effect on filtering. A larger damping value preserves edges better but smooths less, while a smaller value produces more smoothing. This parameter is only valid when the filter_type argument is set to EnhancedLee or Frost. (默認值為 None)
- Double

## 圖徵集和記錄集 · 圖徵集和記錄集

**id**　c78f9f2625dce7d2a2-1

**english**　Feature sets and record sets

**chapter**　
- Python
- 入門
- 處理資料

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/get-started/featuresets-and-recordsets.html

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/get-started/featuresets-and-recordsets.html

**text**　FeatureSet 對象是圖徵類的輕量級表示。 FeatureSet 對象是特殊的資料元素，其中不僅包含方案（幾何類型、欄位和空間參考），還包含資料（包括幾何）。 RecordSet 對象與之相似，但形式類似于表格。 在腳本工具中使用時，圖徵集和記錄集可以用于以交互方式定義圖徵和記錄。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/get-started/featuresets-and-recordsets.html

**id**　c78f9f2625dce7d2a2-2

**english**　Feature sets and record sets

**text**　服務器工具使用圖徵集和記錄集進行通信，這意味著在使用服務器工具時，必須使用這些對象創建資料或將資料加載到這些對象中。
FeatureSet 和 RecordSet 類具有兩個相同的方法。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/get-started/featuresets-and-recordsets.html
